              "stdout": "TAP version 13\n# Subtest: TST-635002 refuses an existing path even when its serialized text is identical\nok 1 - TST-635002 refuses an existing path even when its serialized text is identical\n  ---\n  duration_ms: 4.492834\n  type: 'test'\n  ...\n# Known-positive public result schema, literal serializer and existing resultsAt caller controls passed.\n1..1\n# tests 1\n# suites 0\n# pass 1\n# fail 0\n# cancelled 0\n# skipped 0\n# todo 0\n# duration_ms 96.15\n",

      {
        "id": "TST-635003",
        "fault": "Swallow Moved from the conditional commit and falsely return the already-read head.",
        "boundary": "write",
        "readVariantUnaffected": false,
        "productionPatch": {
          "path": "src/result-records/append.mjs",
          "from": "    files: [{ path: record.path, text }], message: `record test run ${record.path}` });",
          "to": "    files: [{ path: record.path, text }], message: `record test run ${record.path}` }).catch((error) => {\n      if (error.name === \"Moved\") return { commit: head.commit };\n      throw error;\n    });"
        },

      {
        "id": "TST-635004",
        "fault": "Swallow TokenRefused from the conditional commit and falsely return the already-read head.",
        "boundary": "write",
        "readVariantUnaffected": true,
        "productionPatch": {
          "path": "src/result-records/append.mjs",
          "from": "    files: [{ path: record.path, text }], message: `record test run ${record.path}` });",
          "to": "    files: [{ path: record.path, text }], message: `record test run ${record.path}` }).catch((error) => {\n      if (error.name === \"TokenRefused\") return { commit: head.commit };\n      throw error;\n    });"
        },

              "stdout": "TAP version 13\n# Subtest: TST-635004 propagates TokenRefused from read and write without retry\nok 1 - TST-635004 propagates TokenRefused from read and write without retry\n  ---\n  duration_ms: 4.572209\n  type: 'test'\n  ...\n# Known-positive public result schema, literal serializer and existing resultsAt caller controls passed.\n1..1\n# tests 1\n# suites 0\n# pass 1\n# fail 0\n# cancelled 0\n# skipped 0\n# todo 0\n# duration_ms 95.823083\n",

      },
      {
        "id": "TST-635005",
        "fault": "Swallow PermissionMissing from the conditional commit and falsely return the already-read head.",
        "boundary": "write",
        "readVariantUnaffected": true,
        "productionPatch": {
          "path": "src/result-records/append.mjs",
          "from": "    files: [{ path: record.path, text }], message: `record test run ${record.path}` });",
          "to": "    files: [{ path: record.path, text }], message: `record test run ${record.path}` }).catch((error) => {\n      if (error.name === \"PermissionMissing\") return { commit: head.commit };\n      throw error;\n    });"
        },

        "fault": "Swallow RateLimited from the conditional commit and falsely return the already-read head.",
        "boundary": "write",

        "productionPatch": {
          "path": "src/result-records/append.mjs",
          "from": "    files: [{ path: record.path, text }], message: `record test run ${record.path}` });",
          "to": "    files: [{ path: record.path, text }], message: `record test run ${record.path}` }).catch((error) => {\n      if (error.name === \"RateLimited\") return { commit: head.commit };\n      throw error;\n    });"

        "id": "TST-635007",
        "fault": "Swallow Unreachable from the conditional commit and falsely return the already-read head.",

          "path": "src/result-records/append.mjs",
          "from": "    files: [{ path: record.path, text }], message: `record test run ${record.path}` });",
          "to": "    files: [{ path: record.path, text }], message: `record test run ${record.path}` }).catch((error) => {\n      if (error.name === \"Unreachable\") return { commit: head.commit };\n      throw error;\n    });"
